    xindex = xoffset + tl.arange(0, XBLOCK)[:]
    xmask = tl.full([XBLOCK], True, tl.int1)[:]
    x0 = (xindex % 128)
    x1 = ((xindex // 128) % 96)
    x2 = xindex // 12288
    x3 = xindex
    tmp0 = tl.load(in_ptr0 + (x0 + 128*(x2 // 4) + 1024*x1), None).to(tl.float32)
    tl.store(out_ptr0 + (x3), tmp0, None)
''', device_str='cuda')
